===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\components\EngineeringNode.tsx =====
"use client";

import { memo } from "react";
import { Handle, Position, type NodeProps } from "@xyflow/react";
import { IconChevronDown, IconChevronRight, IconCube, IconStack2 } from "@tabler/icons-react";
import type { LatticeFlowNode } from "../canvas/lattice-flow-types";

function EngineeringNodeComponent({ data }: NodeProps<LatticeFlowNode>) {
  const Icon = data.entityKind === "assembly" ? IconStack2 : IconCube;
  return (
    <article
      aria-label={`${data.entityKind ?? data.category} ${data.label}, source ${data.source}, ${data.hasChildren ? (data.expanded ? "expanded" : "collapsed") : "leaf"}`}
      className={`group relative w-64 rounded-xl border bg-slate-950/95 p-3 text-slate-100 shadow-xl transition ${data.selected ? "border-cyan-400 ring-2 ring-cyan-400/20" : "border-slate-700 focus-within:border-slate-500"}`}
    >
      <Handle id="in" type="target" position={Position.Left} className="!h-2.5 !w-2.5 !border-2 !border-slate-950 !bg-slate-400" />
      <Handle id="out" type="source" position={Position.Right} className="!h-2.5 !w-2.5 !border-2 !border-slate-950 !bg-cyan-400" />
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <span className="mt-0.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-2 text-cyan-300"><Icon className="h-4 w-4" /></span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">{data.label}</p>
          <p className="mt-1 truncate text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">{data.subtitle}</p>
        </div>
        {data.hasChildren ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            data-expand-node={data.id}
            aria-label={`${data.expanded ? "Collapse" : "Expand"} ${data.label}`}
            aria-expanded={data.expanded}
            className="nodrag nopan rounded-md p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-cyan-300"
          >
            {data.expanded ? <IconChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" /> : <IconChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />}
            <span className="sr-only">{data.hiddenChildren} hidden children</span>
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

export const EngineeringNode = memo(EngineeringNodeComponent);

"use client";
import { memo, useEffect, useState, type ComponentType, type MouseEvent, type ReactNode } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import {
  Handle,
  NodeToolbar,
  Position,
  useUpdateNodeInternals,
  type NodeProps,
} from "@xyflow/react";
import { LayoutGroup, motion, useReducedMotion } from "motion/react";
import {
  IconAlertTriangle,
  IconBuildingBank,
  IconChartHistogram,
  IconHistory,
  IconPackages,
  IconAtom,
  IconBox,
  IconChevronRight,
  IconClipboardCheck,
  IconFlask,
  IconFocus2,
  IconGitCompare,
  IconHierarchy3,
  IconInfoCircle,
  IconPinnedOff,
  IconRulerMeasure,
  IconScale,
  IconVersions,
  IconX,
} from "@tabler/icons-react";
import type {
  LatticeFlowNode,
  RelationshipFamily,
} from "../canvas/lattice-flow-types";
import type { LatticeNodeAction } from "../canvas/lattice-node-actions";
import {
  directionVector,
  nodeVariants,
  valueVariants,
} from "../motion/lattice-motion";
const tones: Record<string, string> = {
  teamcenter: "cyan",
  windchill: "green",
  sap: "sky",
  configit: "fuchsia",
  unified: "slate",
};
const sourceLogos: Partial<Record<string, { src: string; alt: string }>> = {
  windchill: { src: "/images/WC_Logo.png", alt: "Windchill" },
  sap: { src: "/images/Sap_Logo.png", alt: "SAP" },
  configit: { src: "/images/configit_Logo.png", alt: "Configit" },
};
function SourceVisual({
  source,
  leaf,
  Icon,
}: {
  source: string;
  leaf: boolean;
  Icon: ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}) {
  const logo = leaf ? sourceLogos[source] : undefined;
  return logo ? (
    <Image
      src={logo.src}
      alt={`${logo.alt} source`}
      width={38}
      height={38}
      className="lattice-source-logo"
    />
  ) : (
    <Icon />
  );
}
const actions: {
  action: LatticeNodeAction;
  label: string;
  icon: ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
}[] = [
  { action: "focus", label: "Focus branch", icon: IconFocus2 },
  { action: "expand-one", label: "Expand one level", icon: IconChevronRight },
  { action: "collapse-descendants", label: "Collapse branch", icon: IconX },
  { action: "open-details", label: "Open details", icon: IconInfoCircle },
  { action: "reset-position", label: "Reset position", icon: IconPinnedOff },
];
function Toolbar({ id, data }: { id: string; data: LatticeFlowNode["data"] }) {
  return (
    <NodeToolbar isVisible={data.selected} position={Position.Top} offset={10}>
      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
        className="lattice-node-toolbar nodrag nopan nowheel"
        role="toolbar"
        aria-label={`Actions for ${data.label}`}
      >
        {actions
          .filter(
            (item) =>
              data.hasChildren ||
              !["expand-one", "collapse-descendants"].includes(item.action),
          )
          .map(({ action, label, icon: Icon }) => (
            <motion.button
              whileHover={{ y: -1 }}
              whileTap={{ scale: 0.96 }}
              whileFocus={{ outline: "2px solid #94a3b8" }}
              key={action}
              type="button"
              data-node-action={action}
              data-node-id={id}
              title={label}
              aria-label={label}
            >
              <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </motion.button>
          ))}
      </motion.div>
    </NodeToolbar>
  );
}
const handlePosition = {
  NORTH: Position.Top,
  EAST: Position.Right,
  SOUTH: Position.Bottom,
  WEST: Position.Left,
} as const;
function Handles({ data }: { data: LatticeFlowNode["data"] }) {
  const configured = (
    data.relationshipFamilies?.length
      ? data.relationshipFamilies
      : ["structure"]
  ) as RelationshipFamily[];
  const families = [
    ...new Set<RelationshipFamily>(["structure", ...configured]),
  ];
  const sides = [
    { name: "north", position: Position.Top },
    { name: "east", position: Position.Right },
    { name: "south", position: Position.Bottom },
    { name: "west", position: Position.Left },
  ] as const;
  return (
    <>
      {families.flatMap((family) =>
        sides.flatMap((side) => [
          <Handle
            key={`target:${side.name}:${family}`}
            id={`target:${side.name}:${family}`}
            type="target"
            position={side.position}
            className={`lattice-handle family-${family}`}
          />,
          <Handle
            key={`source:${side.name}:${family}`}
            id={`source:${side.name}:${family}`}
            type="source"
            position={side.position}
            className={`lattice-handle family-${family}`}
          />,
        ]),
      )}
    </>
  );
}
function Chrome({
  id,
  data,
  children,
  geometrySignal = 0,
}: {
  id: string;
  data: LatticeFlowNode["data"];
  children: ReactNode;
  geometrySignal?: number;
}) {
  const update = useUpdateNodeInternals();
  useEffect(() => {
    let second = 0;
    const first = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      update(id);
      second = requestAnimationFrame(() => update(id));
    });
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(first);
      if (second) cancelAnimationFrame(second);
    };
  }, [id, data.geometryRevision, data.expanded, geometrySignal, update]);
  return (
    <>
      <Toolbar id={id} data={data} />
      <Handles data={data} />
      {children}
    </>
  );
}
const icons: Record<string, ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  material: IconAtom,
  chemical: IconFlask,
  weight: IconScale,
  dimension: IconRulerMeasure,
  version: IconVersions,
  state: IconInfoCircle,
  view: IconHierarchy3,
  source: IconBox,
};
function Shell({
  id,
  data,
  children,
  className = "",
  geometrySignal = 0,
}: {
  id: string;
  data: LatticeFlowNode["data"];
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  geometrySignal?: number;
}) {
  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
  const direction = directionVector(data.layoutDirection);
  return (
    <motion.article
      custom={direction}
      variants={nodeVariants}
      initial={
        reduced
          ? { opacity: 0 }
          : { opacity: 0, x: direction.x * 12, y: direction.y * 12 }
      }
      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0, y: 0 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0, x: direction.x * 8, y: direction.y * 8 }}
      onAnimationStart={() =>
        window.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent("lattice:geometry-motion", {
            detail: { active: true, id },
          }),
        )
      }
      onAnimationComplete={() =>
        window.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent("lattice:geometry-motion", {
            detail: { active: false, id },
          }),
        )
      }
      onLayoutAnimationStart={() =>
        window.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent("lattice:geometry-motion", {
            detail: { active: true, id },
          }),
        )
      }
      onLayoutAnimationComplete={() =>
        window.dispatchEvent(
          new CustomEvent("lattice:geometry-motion", {
            detail: { active: false, id },
          }),
        )
      }
      whileHover={reduced ? undefined : ({ y: -1 } as never)}
      className={`lattice-premium-node relative flex h-full w-full min-w-0 items-start gap-4 overflow-visible rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-950/95 px-5 py-4 text-slate-100 shadow-xl ${className} ${data.selected ? "is-selected" : ""} ${data.dimmed ? "is-dimmed" : ""}`}
      style={
        {
          "--accent-strength": data.selected ? 1 : 0.62,
          "--border-opacity": data.selected ? 0.9 : 0.46,
        } as never
      }
    >
      <Chrome id={id} data={data} geometrySignal={geometrySignal}>
        {children}
      </Chrome>
    </motion.article>
  );
}
function Subject({ id, data }: { id:string; data:LatticeFlowNode["data"] }) {
  return (
    <Shell id={id} data={data} className="lattice-subject-node">
      <div className="source-icon">
        <IconHierarchy3 />
      </div>
      <h2>{data.label}</h2>
    </Shell>
  );
}
const domainIcons: Record<string, ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  "physical-attributes": IconAtom,

  requirements: IconClipboardCheck,
  changes: IconGitCompare,

function Domain({ id, data }: { id:string; data:LatticeFlowNode["data"] }) {
  const domain = id.replace("projection:domain:", "");
  const Icon = domainIcons[domain] ?? IconHierarchy3;
  return (
    <Shell
      id={id}
      data={data}
      className={`lattice-domain-node domain-${domain}`}
    >
      <div className="source-icon">
        <Icon />
      </div>
      <h3>{data.label}</h3>
      {data.hasChildren ? (
        <motion.button
          whileHover={{ y: -1 }}
          whileTap={{ scale: 0.96 }}
          whileFocus={{ outline: "2px solid #94a3b8" }}
          type="button"
          data-expand-node={id}
          className="lattice-disclosure nodrag nopan nowheel"
          aria-expanded={data.expanded}
        >
          {data.expanded ? "Collapse" : "Explore"}
        </motion.button>
      ) : null}
    </Shell>
  );
}
function Entity({ id, data }: { id:string; data:LatticeFlowNode["data"] }) {
  if (id.startsWith("projection:domain:"))
    return <Domain id={id} data={data} />;
  const kind = String(data.entityKind ?? data.category);
  const visualSource = String(data.inheritedSource ?? data.source);
  const showSourceLogo = Boolean(sourceLogos[visualSource]) && (!data.hasChildren || Boolean(data.inheritedSource));
  const key = String(data.attributes?.attributeKey ?? data.label).toLowerCase();
  const Icon =
    icons[key] ??
    (kind === "requirement"
      ? IconClipboardCheck
      : kind === "change-notice"
        ? IconAlertTriangle
        : kind === "change-task"
          ? IconGitCompare
          : IconBox);
  const rawValue = data.attributes?.value;
  const value =
    rawValue !== undefined &&
    rawValue !== null &&
    String(rawValue).trim() !== ""
      ? String(rawValue)
      : "Unavailable";

    typeof data.attributes?.requirementId === "string"
      ? data.attributes.requirementId
      : kind === "requirement" && data.nativeIdentifier
        ? data.nativeIdentifier

¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¦ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¦ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€¦Ã‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â:]\s*/, "")
      : data.label;
  return (
    <Shell
      id={id}
      data={data}
      className={`lattice-entity-node tone-${visualSource === "configit" ? "orange" : tones[visualSource] ?? "slate"} ${isCodebeamer ? "is-codebeamer" : ""} ${isSelectedCodebeamerRequirement ? "is-codebeamer-focus" : ""} ${isDetailGroup ? "is-detail-group" : ""} ${isDescription ? "is-description-leaf" : ""}`}
      geometrySignal={showFullValue ? 1 : 0}
    >
      <LayoutGroup id={`node-${id}`}>
        <div className={`source-icon mt-0.5 flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-600/80 bg-slate-800/80 text-slate-300 ${showSourceLogo ? "has-source-logo" : ""}`}>
          <SourceVisual source={visualSource} leaf={showSourceLogo} Icon={Icon} />
        </div>
        <div className="node-copy min-w-0 flex-1 self-stretch">
          {isDetailGroup ? (
            <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col justify-center">
              <span className="node-field text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-cyan-300/80">{data.subtitle}</span>
              <h3 className="mt-1 truncate text-base font-semibold leading-6 text-slate-50">{data.label}</h3>
            </div>
          ) : isRequirementDetail ? (
            <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
              <span className="node-field shrink-0 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-cyan-300/80">{data.label}</span>
              <motion.div variants={valueVariants} initial="hidden" animate="visible" className="mt-2 min-h-0 flex-1">
                <p
                  className={`node-value break-words text-left text-slate-50 ${isDescription ? "text-[15px] font-medium leading-6" : "text-lg font-semibold leading-7"} ${showFullValue ? "max-h-[8.5rem] overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain pr-2 whitespace-pre-line" : "overflow-hidden"}`}
                  style={showFullValue ? undefined : { display: "-webkit-box", WebkitLineClamp: isDescription ? 3 : 2, WebkitBoxOrient: "vertical" }}
                >
                  {value}
                </p>
              </motion.div>
              {isDescription && value.length > 140 ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="nodrag nopan mt-2 inline-flex w-fit shrink-0 items-center rounded-md border border-cyan-400/20 bg-cyan-400/[0.06] px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-cyan-300 transition-colors hover:border-cyan-300/40 hover:bg-cyan-400/10 hover:text-cyan-100"
                  aria-expanded={showFullValue}
                  aria-label={`${showFullValue ? "Collapse" : "Read full"} description`}
                  onClick={(event: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
                    event.stopPropagation();
                    setShowFullValue((current: boolean) => !current);
                  }}
                >
                  {showFullValue ? "Show less" : "Read more"}
                </button>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          ) : isCodebeamer ? (
            <>
              <span className="node-field">{data.subtitle}</span>
              <h3>{data.label}</h3>
            </>
          ) : requirementId ? (
            <>
              <span className="node-identifier">{requirementId}</span>
              <h3>{title || data.label}</h3>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <span className="node-field">{data.label}</span>
              {![
                "physical-attribute",
                "product-context",
                "product-detail",
                "stock-metric",
                "history-metric",
                "sap-field",
              ].includes(kind) && !data.attributes?.value ? (
                <h3>{data.label}</h3>
              ) : null}
            </>
          )}
          {!isRequirementDetail && (!isCodebeamer && [
            "physical-attribute",
            "product-context",
            "product-detail",
            "stock-metric",
            "history-metric",
            "sap-field",
          ].includes(kind) || data.attributes?.value) ? (
            <motion.p
              variants={valueVariants}
              initial="hidden"
              animate="visible"
              className={`node-value ${value === "Unavailable" ? "is-unavailable" : ""}`}
            >
              {value}
            </motion.p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </LayoutGroup>
      {data.hasChildren ? (
        <motion.button
          whileTap={{ scale: 0.96 }}
          type="button"
          data-expand-node={id}
          className="lattice-disclosure nodrag nopan nowheel"
          aria-expanded={data.expanded}
        >
          {data.expanded ? "Collapse" : "Explore"}
        </motion.button>
      ) : null}
    </Shell>
  );
}
export const InvestigationSubjectNode = memo(Subject);
export const SourceRepresentationNodeView = memo(Entity);
export const PremiumEngineeringNode = memo(Entity);

export type NodeZoomTier = "identity" | "working" | "detail";
export function nodeZoomTier(zoom:number):NodeZoomTier { return zoom < 0.55 ? "identity" : zoom < 0.9 ? "working" : "detail"; }

import type { EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1, IntelligenceProperty } from "../contracts/intelligence-v1";

const sourceOf=(value?:string):EntitySource=>value==="teamcenter"||value==="windchill"||value==="sap"||value==="configit"||value==="codebeamer"?value:"platform";
const primitive=(value:unknown):string|number|boolean|undefined=>{
 if(value===null||value===undefined)return undefined;
 if(typeof value==="string"||typeof value==="number"||typeof value==="boolean")return value;
 if(Array.isArray(value))return value.map(item=>primitive(item)).filter(item=>item!==undefined).join(", ");
 if(typeof value==="object"){
  const record=value as Record<string,unknown>;
  for(const key of ["displayValue","value","label","text","name","formattedValue"]){const resolved=primitive(record[key]);if(resolved!==undefined&&String(resolved).trim())return resolved;}
 }
 return undefined;
};
const scalar=(value:IntelligenceProperty):string|number|boolean|undefined=>primitive(value.text)??primitive(value.number)??primitive(value.boolean)??primitive(value.timestamp)??primitive(value.reference)??primitive(value.items);
const attributes=(properties:Record<string,IntelligenceProperty>)=>Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(properties).sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b)).flatMap(([key,value])=>{const resolved=scalar(value);return resolved===undefined?[]:[[key,resolved]]}));
const confidence=(value?:string)=>value==="verified"?1:value==="deterministic"?.9:value==="probable"?.72:value==="ambiguous"?.45:value==="unresolved"?.2:undefined;
const kind=(value:string):EntityKind=>value as EntityKind;
const relationshipKind=(value:string):RelationshipKind=>value as RelationshipKind;

export function buildIntelligenceInvestigation(input:EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1):InvestigationGraph{

 const deduplicated=deduplicateIntelligenceEntities(input);
 const evidenceById=Object.fromEntries(input.evidence.map(item=>[item.id,item]));
 const clustersByEntity=new Map<string,string[]>();
 for(const cluster of input.identityClusters)for(const entityId of cluster.entityIds)clustersByEntity.set(entityId,[...(clustersByEntity.get(entityId)??[]),cluster.id]);
 const subjectSourceIds=input.sources.map(item=>item.entityId).filter(Boolean);
 const subject:EngineeringEntity={id:input.subject.id,sourceNodeId:input.subject.preferredProductId??input.subject.id,source:"unified",name:input.subject.displayName,kind:"identity",level:0,attributes:{"Preferred product ID":input.subject.preferredProductId??"","Candidate IDs":input.subject.candidateIds.join(", "),"Source coverage":input.summary.availableSourceCount,"Partial sources":input.summary.partialSourceCount,"Investigation status":input.summary.status,"Evidence completeness":input.sources.length?`${input.summary.availableSourceCount}/${input.sources.length} sources available`:"not observed","Contract version":input.contractVersion},provenance:{sourceLabel:input.subject.provenance.provider,nativeId:input.subject.provenance.nativeId,capturedAt:input.subject.provenance.observedAt},representationSources:input.sources.map(x=>x.source).filter((x):x is "teamcenter"|"windchill"|"sap"|"configit"=>x==="teamcenter"||x==="windchill"||x==="sap"||x==="configit").sort(),canonicalKind:"part",assertion:"fact",temporal:{assembledAt:input.subject.temporal.assembledAt,capturedAt:input.subject.temporal.capturedAt,freshness:input.subject.temporal.freshness},identityClusterIds:clustersByEntity.get(input.subject.id)??[]};
 const levels=new Map<string,number>([[subject.id,0]]);for(const id of subjectSourceIds)levels.set(id,1);
 const entities:EngineeringEntity[]=[subject,...deduplicated.entities.map(entity=>({id:entity.id,sourceNodeId:entity.provenance.nativeId??entity.id,source:sourceOf(entity.provenance.source),name:entity.displayName,kind:kind(entity.kind),level:levels.get(entity.id)??1,attributes:attributes(entity.properties),provenance:{sourceLabel:entity.provenance.provider,nativeId:entity.provenance.nativeId,capturedAt:entity.provenance.observedAt},canonicalKind:entity.kind,assertion:entity.assertion,sourceStatus:input.sources.find(x=>x.entityId===entity.id)?.status,temporal:{assembledAt:entity.temporal.assembledAt,capturedAt:entity.temporal.capturedAt,freshness:entity.temporal.freshness},identityClusterIds:clustersByEntity.get(entity.id)??[]}))];
 const entityIds=new Set(entities.map(x=>x.id));
 const relationships:EngineeringRelationship[]=input.relationships.map(r=>{const from=canonicalEntityId(r.sourceEntityId,deduplicated.aliases),to=canonicalEntityId(r.targetEntityId,deduplicated.aliases);return {...r,sourceEntityId:from,targetEntityId:to}}).filter(r=>entityIds.has(r.sourceEntityId)&&entityIds.has(r.targetEntityId)).map(r=>({id:r.id,from:r.sourceEntityId,to:r.targetEntityId,kind:relationshipKind(r.kind),confidence:confidence(r.confidence),verified:r.confidence==="verified",label:r.kind.replaceAll("-"," "),evidence:r.evidenceIds.map(id=>evidenceById[id]).filter(Boolean).map(item=>({sourceLabel:item.source,reason:item.summary,nativeId:item.provenance.nativeId,capturedAt:item.provenance.observedAt})),family:r.family,assertion:r.assertion,evidenceIds:[...r.evidenceIds].sort()}));
 for(const finding of [...input.findings].sort((a,b)=>a.id.localeCompare(b.id))){const findingId=`entity:${finding.id}`;entities.push({id:findingId,sourceNodeId:finding.id,source:"platform",name:finding.title,kind:"finding",level:1,attributes:{Summary:finding.summary},provenance:{sourceLabel:finding.provenance.provider,nativeId:finding.provenance.nativeId,capturedAt:finding.provenance.observedAt},canonicalKind:"finding",assertion:"finding",temporal:{assembledAt:finding.temporal.assembledAt,capturedAt:finding.temporal.capturedAt,freshness:finding.temporal.freshness},evidenceIds:[...finding.evidenceIds].sort()});for(const rawTarget of [...finding.subjectEntityIds].sort()){const target=canonicalEntityId(rawTarget,deduplicated.aliases);if(entityIds.has(target)){relationships.push({id:`rel:${finding.id}:${target}`,from:findingId,to:target,kind:"supported-by",label:"supported by",evidence:finding.evidenceIds.map(id=>evidenceById[id]).filter(Boolean).map(item=>({sourceLabel:item.source,reason:item.summary,capturedAt:item.provenance.observedAt})),family:"evidence",assertion:"finding",evidenceIds:[...finding.evidenceIds].sort()});}}}

 return immutableInvestigationGraph({entities:sortedEntities,relationships:sortedRelationships,roots:[subject.id],byId:Object.fromEntries(sortedEntities.map(x=>[x.id,x])),relationshipById:Object.fromEntries(sortedRelationships.map(x=>[x.id,x])),metadata:{investigationId:input.investigationId,contractVersion:input.contractVersion,sourceStatuses:Object.fromEntries(input.sources.map(x=>[x.source,x.status]).sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b))),identityClusters:input.identityClusters.map(c=>({id:c.id,entityIds:[...c.entityIds].sort(),confidence:c.confidence,reasons:[...c.reasons].sort()})).sort((a,b)=>a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),evidence:Object.fromEntries(input.evidence.map(e=>[e.id,{id:e.id,kind:e.kind,summary:e.summary,source:e.source}] as const).sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b))),findings:input.findings.map(f=>({id:f.id,kind:f.kind,title:f.title,summary:f.summary,subjectEntityIds:[...f.subjectEntityIds].sort(),evidenceIds:[...f.evidenceIds].sort()})).sort((a,b)=>a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),entityAliases:deduplicated.aliases}});
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\infrastructure\merge-source-intelligence.ts =====
import type { EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1, IntelligenceEntity, IntelligenceRelationship } from "../contracts/intelligence-v1";
import { resolveCrossSourceRelationships } from "./resolve-cross-source-relationships";
const dedupe=<T extends{id:string}>(items:T[])=>[...new Map(items.map(item=>[item.id,item])).values()];
const stableKey=(values:readonly string[]):string=>[...new Set(values)].sort().join("|").replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9:_-]+/g,"-").slice(0,180);
const comparableId=(value:string)=>value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,"");
const subjectEntity=(part:EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1):IntelligenceEntity=>({id:part.subject.id,kind:"source-representation",displayName:part.subject.displayName,identityQuality:"native",assertion:"fact",properties:{preferredProductId:{type:"text",text:part.subject.preferredProductId??""},sourceRole:{type:"text",text:"participating-source-subject"}},provenance:part.subject.provenance,temporal:part.subject.temporal});
export function mergeSourceIntelligence(parts: EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1[]): EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1 {
 if(!parts.length) throw new Error("No source intelligence was returned."); if(parts.length===1) return parts[0];
 const ordered=[...parts].sort((a,b)=>(a.sources[0]?.source??"").localeCompare(b.sources[0]?.source??"")||a.investigationId.localeCompare(b.investigationId)),base=ordered[0];
 const originalSubjects=ordered.map(subjectEntity);
 const entities=dedupe([...originalSubjects,...ordered.flatMap(item=>item.entities)]);
 const canonicalByComparable=new Map<string,string>();for(const entity of entities){const key=comparableId(entity.id);if(!canonicalByComparable.has(key))canonicalByComparable.set(key,entity.id)}
 const canonicalEndpoint=(id:string)=>canonicalByComparable.get(comparableId(id))??id;
 const normalizeRelationship=(relationship:IntelligenceRelationship):IntelligenceRelationship=>({...relationship,sourceEntityId:canonicalEndpoint(relationship.sourceEntityId),targetEntityId:canonicalEndpoint(relationship.targetEntityId)});
 const nativeRelationships=dedupe(ordered.flatMap(item=>item.relationships).filter(relationship=>!(relationship.kind==="represented-by"&&relationship.sourceEntityId.startsWith("investigation-subject:"))).map(normalizeRelationship));
 const evidence=dedupe(ordered.flatMap(item=>item.evidence)),findings=dedupe(ordered.flatMap(item=>item.findings).map(finding=>({...finding,subjectEntityIds:finding.subjectEntityIds.map(canonicalEndpoint)}))),sources=[...new Map(ordered.flatMap(item=>item.sources).map(item=>({...item,entityId:canonicalEndpoint(item.entityId)})).map(item=>[`${item.source}:${item.entityId}`,item])).values()];
 const candidateIds=[...new Set(ordered.flatMap(item=>item.subject.candidateIds).map(canonicalEndpoint))];
 const commonSubject=ordered.every(item=>item.subject.id===base.subject.id);
 const sourceNames=[...new Set(sources.map(item=>item.source))].sort();
 const subjectId=commonSubject?base.subject.id:`investigation-subject:federated:${stableKey(sourceNames)}:${stableKey(candidateIds)}`;
 const displayName=commonSubject?base.subject.displayName:ordered.map(item=>item.subject.displayName).filter((value,index,all)=>all.indexOf(value)===index).join(" + ");
 const federationProvenance={...base.subject.provenance,nativeId:undefined,artifactReference:subjectId,provider:"lattice-canonical-merge",authority:"backend-derived" as const};
 const identityClusters=dedupe(ordered.flatMap(item=>item.identityClusters).map(cluster=>({...cluster,entityIds:cluster.entityIds.map(canonicalEndpoint),preferredEntityId:cluster.preferredEntityId?canonicalEndpoint(cluster.preferredEntityId):undefined})));
 const provisional:EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1={...base,investigationId:`federated-${stableKey(ordered.map(item=>item.investigationId))}`,subject:{...base.subject,id:subjectId,displayName,candidateIds,reasons:[...new Set(ordered.flatMap(item=>item.subject.reasons))],provenance:federationProvenance},entities,relationships:nativeRelationships,evidence,findings,sources,identityClusters,temporal:{...base.temporal,assembledAt:new Date().toISOString()},summary:{entityCount:entities.length,relationshipCount:nativeRelationships.length,evidenceCount:evidence.length,findingCount:findings.length,availableSourceCount:sources.filter(item=>item.status==="available").length,partialSourceCount:sources.filter(item=>item.status!=="available").length,status:sources.every(item=>item.status==="available")?"success":"partial-success"}};
 const crossSource=resolveCrossSourceRelationships(provisional);
 const relationships=dedupe([...nativeRelationships,...crossSource]);
 return {...provisional,relationships,summary:{...provisional.summary,relationshipCount:relationships.length}};
}

const relationshipLabel=(kind:string,source:string,target:string)=>{if(target.includes("physical-attributes"))return "physical properties";if(target.includes("product-context"))return "product context";if(target.includes("requirements"))return "defined by";if(target.includes("changes"))return "changed through";if(kind==="described-by")return "described by";if(kind==="supersedes")return "supersedes";if(kind==="affected-by")return "affected by";if(kind==="contains"&&target.includes("requirement"))return "contains requirement";if(kind==="requirement")return "defined by";return undefined};
const relationshipFamily=(kind:RelationshipProjection["edges"][number]["kind"]):RelationshipFamily=>kind==="contains"?"structure":kind==="requirement"?"requirement":kind==="business-impact"?"operational":kind==="corresponds-to"||kind==="comparison"?"correspondence":"evidence";

function resolution(value?:string):ResolutionState{return value==="failed"?"failed":value==="partial"?"partial":value==="warning"?"warning":value==="resolving"||value==="extracting"?"resolving":"ready"}
export function toReactFlow(projection:RelationshipProjection,positions:Record<string,{x:number;y:number}>,pinned:LatticePinnedPositions={},orientation:LayoutOrientation="RIGHT",layout?:UniversalLayoutResult):{nodes:LatticeFlowNode[];edges:LatticeFlowEdge[]}{
 const branchDirection=new Map(layout?.branches.flatMap(branch=>branch.nodeIds.map(id=>[id,branch.direction] as const))??[]);
 const incoming=new Map<string,RelationshipProjection["edges"]>();const outgoing=new Map<string,RelationshipProjection["edges"]>();
 for(const edge of projection.edges){incoming.set(edge.target,[...(incoming.get(edge.target)??[]),edge]);outgoing.set(edge.source,[...(outgoing.get(edge.source)??[]),edge])}
 const flowNodes=projection.nodes.map((node,index)=>{
  const dimensions=projectedNodeDimensions(node);
  const represented=(incoming.get(node.id)??[]).some(edge=>edge.kind==="represented-by");
  const domain=node.id.startsWith("projection:domain:");
  const category:LatticeNodeCategory=node.level===0?"subject":domain?"cluster":represented?"source-representation":node.kind==="requirement"?"requirement":node.kind==="change-notice"||node.kind==="change-task"?"change":node.kind==="finding"||node.kind==="document"?"evidence":"engineering-item";
  const inheritedSource=node.level>1&&!domain&&node.hasChildren?node.inheritedSource:undefined;
  const attributes=node.attributes??{};
  const completeness=Number(attributes.Completeness??attributes.completeness??(node.resolutionState==="partial"?65:100));
  const families=[...new Set([...(incoming.get(node.id)??[]),...(outgoing.get(node.id)??[])].map(edge=>relationshipFamily(edge.kind)))];
  return {
    id:node.id,
    type:category,
    position:pinned[node.id]??positions[node.id]??{x:node.level*320,y:0},
    origin:[0,0],
    width:dimensions.width,
    height:dimensions.height,
    style:{width:dimensions.width,height:dimensions.height},
    selected:node.selected,
    data:{category,label:node.label,subtitle:node.subtitle,source:node.source,inheritedSource,level:node.level,selected:node.selected,expanded:node.expanded,hasChildren:node.hasChildren,hiddenChildren:node.hiddenChildren,entityKind:node.kind,nativeIdentifier:node.nativeIdentifier,revision:node.revision,completeness,resolutionState:resolution(node.resolutionState),matchConfidence:node.matchConfidence===undefined?undefined:Math.round(node.matchConfidence*100),childCount:node.childCount,lastCapturedAt:node.capturedAt,participatingSystems:node.provenanceSources?.length??1,coverageSummary:`${node.relationshipCount} relationships`,relationshipFamilies:families.length?families:["structure"],orientation,activeResolution:node.resolutionState==="resolving"||node.resolutionState==="extracting",pinned:Boolean(pinned[node.id]),evidenceSummary:node.subtitle,attributes:node.attributes,entering:true,revealIndex:index,geometryRevision:dimensions.width*1000+dimensions.height,routeSettled:true,dimmed:node.dimmed,layoutDirection:branchDirection.get(node.id),radialHandles:layout?Object.values(layout.ports).filter(port=>port.nodeId===node.id&&Boolean(port.branchId)):undefined}
  } as LatticeFlowNode;
});
 const nodeById=new Map(flowNodes.map(node=>[node.id,node]));
 const flowEdges=projection.edges.map(edge=>{
  const category=edgeCategory(edge.kind),family=relationshipFamily(edge.kind);
  const source=nodeById.get(edge.source),target=nodeById.get(edge.target);
  const sourceCenter={x:(source?.position.x??0)+(source?.width??196)/2,y:(source?.position.y??0)+(source?.height??76)/2};
  const targetCenter={x:(target?.position.x??0)+(target?.width??196)/2,y:(target?.position.y??0)+(target?.height??76)/2};
  const dx=targetCenter.x-sourceCenter.x,dy=targetCenter.y-sourceCenter.y;
  const horizontal=Math.abs(dx)>=Math.abs(dy);
  const sourceSide=horizontal?(dx>=0?"east":"west"):(dy>=0?"south":"north");
  const targetSide=horizontal?(dx>=0?"west":"east"):(dy>=0?"north":"south");
  const label=edge.label||relationshipLabel(edge.kind,edge.source,edge.target);
  return {id:edge.id,source:edge.source,target:edge.target,sourceHandle:`source:${sourceSide}:${family}`,targetHandle:`target:${targetSide}:${family}`,type:"lattice-edge",selectable:true,focusable:true,deletable:false,reconnectable:false,selected:edge.selected,interactionWidth:26,ariaLabel:`${edge.fullLabel??label??edge.kind} relationship`,data:{category,relationshipKind:edge.kind,label,fullLabel:edge.fullLabel||label,confidence:edge.confidence,direction:edge.kind==="corresponds-to"?"bidirectional":(edge.confidence??1)<.7?"inferred":"forward",importance:edge.kind==="contains"?"primary":(edge.confidence??1)<.65?"low":"normal",animation:edge.selected?"selected":"settled",authoritative:true,layoutRoute:undefined}} as LatticeFlowEdge;
 });
 return {nodes:flowNodes,edges:flowEdges};
}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\motion\node-sizing.ts =====
import type { ProjectedNode } from "../contracts/projection";

const LEAF_MIN_WIDTH = 350;
const LEAF_MAX_WIDTH = 580;
const LEAF_MIN_HEIGHT = 150;

const PARENT_MIN_WIDTH = 590;
const PARENT_MAX_WIDTH = 720;
const PARENT_MIN_HEIGHT = 164;

const DOMAIN_WIDTH = 540;
const DOMAIN_HEIGHT = 112;

const SUBJECT_WIDTH = 520;
const SUBJECT_HEIGHT = 180;

const LEAF_HORIZONTAL_PADDING = 118;
const PARENT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING = 126;

const textWidth = (value: string) =>
  Math.ceil(
    [...value].reduce(
      (sum, char) =>
        sum + (char.charCodeAt(0) > 255 ? 15 : 12.5),
      0,
    ),
  );

const lineCount = (
  value: string,
  availableWidth: number,
  maximumLines = 2,
) => {
  if (!value.trim()) return 0;
  return Math.max(
    1,
    Math.min(maximumLines, Math.ceil(textWidth(value) / availableWidth)),
  );
};

export function projectedNodeDimensions(node: ProjectedNode) {
  const value = typeof node.attributes?.value === "string" ? node.attributes.value : "";
  const identifier = typeof node.attributes?.requirementId === "string" ? node.attributes.requirementId : (node.nativeIdentifier ?? "");
  const role = String(node.attributes?.presentationRole ?? "");
  const isDomain = node.id.startsWith("projection:domain:");
  const isSubject = node.level === 0;
  const isGroup = role === "detail-group";
  const isDescription = role === "description";
  const isDetailLeaf = ["detail-field", "description", "custom-field", "attachment", "view-more"].includes(role);

  if (isSubject) return { width: SUBJECT_WIDTH, height: SUBJECT_HEIGHT };
  if (isDomain) return { width: DOMAIN_WIDTH, height: DOMAIN_HEIGHT };
  if (isGroup) return { width: 360, height: 138 };

  if (isDescription) {
    const normalizedLength = value.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().length;
    const width = normalizedLength > 420 ? 720 : normalizedLength > 220 ? 640 : 560;
    return { width, height: 238 };
  }

  if (isDetailLeaf) {
    const width = Math.max(320, Math.min(520, textWidth([node.label, value].sort((a,b)=>textWidth(b)-textWidth(a))[0] ?? "") + 112));
    return { width, height: value.length > 72 ? 174 : 148 };
  }

  const longest = [node.label, node.subtitle, value, identifier].reduce(
    (current, candidate) => textWidth(String(candidate)) > textWidth(String(current)) ? String(candidate) : String(current),
    "",
  );
  const isParent = node.hasChildren;
  const minWidth = isParent ? PARENT_MIN_WIDTH : LEAF_MIN_WIDTH;
  const maxWidth = isParent ? PARENT_MAX_WIDTH : LEAF_MAX_WIDTH;
  const minHeight = isParent ? PARENT_MIN_HEIGHT : LEAF_MIN_HEIGHT;
  const horizontalPadding = isParent ? PARENT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING : LEAF_HORIZONTAL_PADDING;
  const width = Math.max(minWidth, Math.min(maxWidth, textWidth(longest) + horizontalPadding));
  const availableTextWidth = Math.max(1, width - horizontalPadding);
  const calculatedHeight = 78 + lineCount(node.label, availableTextWidth) * 35 + lineCount(value, availableTextWidth) * 32 + lineCount(identifier, availableTextWidth, 1) * 23;
  return { width, height: Math.max(minHeight, calculatedHeight) };
}

  const role=(entity:EngineeringEntity)=>String(entity.attributes.presentationRole??"");
  const baseRoles=new Set(["project","tracker","parent-requirement","selected-requirement"]);
  const candidates=codebeamerEntities.filter(entity=>entity.kind!=="identity"),candidateIds=new Set(candidates.map(entity=>entity.id));
  const links=graph.relationships.filter(link=>candidateIds.has(link.from)&&candidateIds.has(link.to));
  const children=new Map<string,string[]>();for(const link of links)children.set(link.from,[...(children.get(link.from)??[]),link.to]);
  const visibleIds=new Set(candidates.filter(entity=>baseRoles.has(role(entity))).map(entity=>entity.id));
  const reveal=(id:string)=>{for(const child of children.get(id)??[]){visibleIds.add(child);if(options.expanded.has(child))reveal(child)}};for(const id of [...visibleIds])if(options.expanded.has(id))reveal(id);
  const visible=candidates.filter(entity=>visibleIds.has(entity.id));
  const rank=(entity:EngineeringEntity)=>role(entity)==="project"?0:role(entity)==="tracker"?1:role(entity)==="parent-requirement"?2:role(entity)==="selected-requirement"?3:5;
  const subtitle=(entity:EngineeringEntity)=>role(entity)==="project"?"Codebeamer project":role(entity)==="tracker"?`Requirement tracker - ${String(entity.attributes.trackerId??"")}`.replace(/ - $/,""):role(entity)==="parent-requirement"?"Parent requirement":role(entity)==="selected-requirement"?`${String(entity.attributes.externalKey??entity.attributes.itemId??"Requirement")} - Selected requirement`:role(entity)==="contextual-requirement"?"Contextual requirement":role(entity)==="related-item"?String(entity.attributes.relationType??"Related item"):role(entity)==="attachment"?"Source attachment":role(entity)==="detail-group"?`${String(entity.attributes.itemCount??0)} fields`:role(entity)==="description"?"Requirement description":role(entity)==="detail-field"?String(entity.attributes.valueType??"Source field"):role(entity)==="view-more"?"Additional fields":String(entity.attributes.valueType??"Structured source field");
  const codebeamerNodes:ProjectedNode[]=visible.sort((a,b)=>rank(a)-rank(b)||Number(a.attributes.informationPriority??99)-Number(b.attributes.informationPriority??99)||a.id.localeCompare(b.id)).map(entity=>{const childIds=children.get(entity.id)??[],revealable=childIds.filter(childId=>{const child=graph.byId[childId];return child&&!baseRoles.has(role(child))});return{...projectEntity(entity),label:entity.name,level:rank(entity),subtitle:subtitle(entity),nativeIdentifier:role(entity)==="selected-requirement"?String(entity.attributes.externalKey??entity.attributes.itemId??entity.sourceNodeId):undefined,resolutionState:"ready",expanded:options.expanded.has(entity.id),hasChildren:revealable.length>0,childCount:revealable.length,hiddenChildren:options.expanded.has(entity.id)?0:revealable.length,attributes:{...entity.attributes,presentationRole:role(entity)}}});
  const semantic=(relationship:InvestigationGraph["relationships"][number])=>relationship.label??relationship.kind.replaceAll("-"," ");
  const codebeamerEdges:ProjectedEdge[]=links.filter(link=>visibleIds.has(link.from)&&visibleIds.has(link.to)).map(link=>({id:link.id,source:link.from,target:link.to,kind:"contains",selected:options.selectedId===link.id,label:semantic(link),fullLabel:semantic(link),confidence:link.confidence}));
  return{nodes:codebeamerNodes,edges:codebeamerEdges,structuralKey:codebeamerNodes.map(node=>node.id).sort().join("|")+"::"+codebeamerEdges.map(edge=>edge.id).sort().join("|"),domainIds:[],deferredCounts:Object.fromEntries(codebeamerNodes.filter(node=>node.hasChildren).map(node=>[node.id,node.hiddenChildren]))};
 }
 const subjectExcluded=new Set([subject.id]);const hierarchyKinds=new Set(["contains","has-configuration-family","contains-feature"]);const parentLinks=graph.relationships.filter(r=>hierarchyKinds.has(r.kind));
 const explicitDomain=new Map<string,IntelligenceDomainId>();for(const entity of graph.entities){const domain=domainForEntity(entity.canonicalKind??entity.kind);if(domain)explicitDomain.set(entity.id,domain)}
 const resolveDomain=(entityId:string,seen=new Set<string>()):IntelligenceDomainId|undefined=>{if(explicitDomain.has(entityId))return explicitDomain.get(entityId);if(seen.has(entityId))return undefined;seen.add(entityId);const parent=parentLinks.find(link=>link.to===entityId);return parent?resolveDomain(parent.from,seen):undefined};
 const domainOf=new Map(graph.entities.map(entity=>[entity.id,resolveDomain(entity.id)]));
 for(const definition of INTELLIGENCE_DOMAINS){const members=graph.entities.filter(entity=>{if(subjectExcluded.has(entity.id)||entity.canonicalKind==="source-representation"||domainOf.get(entity.id)!==definition.id)return false;if(definition.id==="rules"&&(entity.canonicalKind??entity.kind)==="configuration-rule-field"&&fieldKey(entity)!=="expression")return false;const parent=parentLinks.find(link=>link.to===entity.id);const parentEntity=parent?graph.byId[parent.from]:undefined;if(parentEntity?.source==="sap"&&(parentEntity.canonicalKind??parentEntity.kind)==="material-movement"&&!movementChildAllowed(entity))return false;return true;});if(!members.length)continue;const memberIds=new Set(members.map(entity=>entity.id));const internal=parentLinks.filter(link=>memberIds.has(link.from)&&memberIds.has(link.to));const children=new Map<string,string[]>();const hasParent=new Set<string>();for(const link of internal){children.set(link.from,[...(children.get(link.from)??[]),link.to]);hasParent.add(link.to)}const roots=members.filter(entity=>!hasParent.has(entity.id));const id=domainNodeId(definition.id);const domainExpanded=options.expanded.has(id);const visible=new Set<string>();const reveal=(entityId:string)=>{visible.add(entityId);if(options.expanded.has(entityId))for(const child of children.get(entityId)??[])reveal(child)};if(domainExpanded)roots.forEach(root=>reveal(root.id));deferred[definition.id]=members.length-visible.size;

}


===== FILE: .\OrchestrationPlatform\frontend\features\lattice-next\workspace\LatticeNextWorkspace.tsx =====
/* eslint-disable react-hooks/set-state-in-effect */
"use client";

import {
  AnimatePresence,
  MotionConfig,
  motion,
  useReducedMotion,
} from "motion/react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useReducer, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import {
  IconAlertTriangle,
  IconArrowLeft,
  IconChevronLeft,
  IconSearch,
  IconX,
} from "@tabler/icons-react";
import { readHandoff } from "../persistence/handoff-store";
import { buildInvestigation } from "../engines/build-investigation";
import { buildIntelligenceInvestigation } from "../engines/build-intelligence-investigation";
import { investigateWindchill } from "../infrastructure/windchill-intelligence-client";
import { investigateSap } from "../infrastructure/sap-intelligence-client";
import { investigateConfigit } from "../infrastructure/configit-intelligence-client";
import { mergeSourceIntelligence } from "../infrastructure/merge-source-intelligence";
import { createCurrentInvestigationRuntime } from "../kernel/infrastructure/current-investigation-runtime";
import { HttpInvestigationAuthority } from "../kernel/infrastructure/http-investigation-authority";
import { asInvestigationId } from "../kernel/contracts/investigation-identity";
import { isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1 } from "../contracts/intelligence-v1";
import type { EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1 } from "../contracts/intelligence-v1";
import { createInvestigationGraphCore } from "../engines/investigation-graph";
import { projectVisibleGraph } from "../engines/project-visible-graph";
import {
  initialInvestigation,
  investigationReducer,
} from "../state/investigation-reducer";
import {
  clearInvestigation,
  loadInvestigation,
  saveInvestigation,
} from "../persistence/investigation-store";
import { RelationshipCanvas } from "../components/RelationshipCanvas";
import { LatticeFlowProvider } from "../canvas/LatticeFlowProvider";
import { EntityInspector } from "../components/EntityInspector";
import { IntelligenceInspector } from "../components/IntelligenceInspector";
import { IntelligenceLegend } from "../components/IntelligenceLegend";
import { RelationshipInspector } from "../components/RelationshipInspector";
import { InvestigationToolbar } from "../components/InvestigationToolbar";
import { downloadHierarchicalLatticeJson } from "../export/hierarchical-json-export";
import type {
  HandoffReadResult,
  LatticeHandoff,
  LatticeSourceEnvelope,
} from "../contracts/handoff";
import { DiscoveryWorkspace } from "../discovery/DiscoveryWorkspace";
import { resolveSelectedStructure } from "../discovery/structure-resolver";
import { buildCorrespondenceCandidates } from "../discovery/identity-resolution";
import {
  createDirectInvestigation,
  handoffInvestigationShell,
} from "../discovery/investigation";
import {
  listCanonicalInvestigations,
  loadCanonicalInvestigation,
  saveCanonicalInvestigation,
} from "../persistence/canonical-investigation-store";

type CanonicalInvestigation = NonNullable<
  ReturnType<typeof loadCanonicalInvestigation>
>;

export function LatticeNextWorkspace() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const params = useSearchParams();
  const handoffId = params.get("handoff");
  const investigationId = params.get("investigation");

  const [direct, setDirect] = useState<LatticeHandoff | null>(null);
  const [directCanonical, setDirectCanonical] =
    useState<EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1 | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [serverRestored, setServerRestored] = useState<{ handoff: LatticeHandoff; canonical: EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1 } | null>(null);
  const [serverRestorePending, setServerRestorePending] = useState(Boolean(investigationId));
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!investigationId || direct) { setServerRestorePending(false); return; }
    let active = true;
    const authority = new HttpInvestigationAuthority();
    void authority.load(asInvestigationId(investigationId)).then((snapshot) => {
      if (!active) return;
      const canonical = snapshot?.canonical?.value;
      if (!snapshot || !isEngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1(canonical)) {
        setServerRestorePending(false);
        return;
      }
      const createdAt = snapshot.createdAt;
      setServerRestored({
        canonical,
        handoff: {
          version: 2,
          handoffId: String(snapshot.identity.investigationId),
          createdAt,
          expiresAt: new Date(Date.parse(createdAt) + 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
          subjectLabel: snapshot.subjectLabel,
          sources: snapshot.selections.map((selection) => ({
            source: selection.source as LatticeSourceEnvelope["source"],
            label: selection.source,
            nativeId: selection.nativeId,
            completeness: snapshot.completeness.failedSources.includes(selection.source) ? "partial" : "complete",
            root: { id: `${selection.source}:${selection.nativeId}`, name: selection.displayName, attributes: { "Item ID": selection.nativeId }, children: [] },
          })),
        },
      });
      setServerRestorePending(false);
    }).catch((error) => {
      if (active) { setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "The durable investigation could not be restored."); setServerRestorePending(false); }
    });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [investigationId, direct]);

  const [handoff] = useState<HandoffReadResult | null>(() =>
    handoffId ? readHandoff(handoffId) : null,
  );
  const [restored] = useState<CanonicalInvestigation | null>(() =>
    investigationId ? loadCanonicalInvestigation(investigationId) : null,
  );

  const startNewInvestigation = useCallback(() => {
    setDirect(null);
    setDirectCanonical(null);
    setMessage(null);
    router.replace("/lattice");
  }, [router]);

  if (direct) {
    return (
      <Investigation
        handoffId={direct.handoffId}
        handoff={direct}
        canonical={directCanonical}
        onStartNew={startNewInvestigation}
      />
    );
  }

  if (serverRestored) {
    return <Investigation handoffId={serverRestored.handoff.handoffId} handoff={serverRestored.handoff} canonical={serverRestored.canonical} onStartNew={startNewInvestigation} />;
  }
  if (serverRestorePending) {
    return <main className="flex min-h-[calc(100vh-64px)] items-center justify-center bg-slate-950 text-sm text-slate-400">Restoring durable investigation...</main>;
  }
  if (handoffId) {
    return handoff?.ok ? (
      <Investigation
        handoffId={handoff.value.handoffId}
        handoff={handoff.value}
        onStartNew={startNewInvestigation}
      />
    ) : (
      <Recovery
        message={handoff?.message ?? "The contextual handoff is unavailable."}
      />
    );
  }

  if (restored) {
    return <Restored canonical={restored} />;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <DiscoveryWorkspace
        onInvestigate={async (selected, subjectLabel) => {
          const runtime = createCurrentInvestigationRuntime();
          const toastId = toast.loading("Creating engineering investigation", { description: "Preparing selected source evidence and opening the governed investigation workflow." });
          try {
            const result = await runtime.investigate(
              selected,
              subjectLabel || selected[0]?.displayName || "Engineering investigation",
            );
            buildIntelligenceInvestigation(result.canonical);
            setDirectCanonical(result.canonical);
            setDirect(result.handoff);
            router.replace(`/lattice?investigation=${encodeURIComponent(String(result.snapshot.identity.investigationId))}`);
            toast.success("Investigation ready", { id: toastId, description: "Canonical evidence passed validation and the investigation workspace is opening." });
            if (result.snapshot.lifecycle === "partial" || result.snapshot.completeness.overall === "partial") {
              const limitation = result.snapshot.completeness.limitations.join(" | ") || "The investigation completed with partial source evidence.";
              setMessage(limitation);
              toast.warning("Investigation opened with partial evidence", { description: limitation });
            }
          } catch (cause) {
            const technicalDetail = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "The Investigation Kernel could not complete the investigation.";
            const detail = /[A-Z]:\\|EPERM|EBUSY|EACCES/i.test(technicalDetail) ? "Investigation persistence is temporarily unavailable. Retry after the file lock clears." : technicalDetail;
            console.error("Lattice investigation failure", cause);
            setMessage(detail);
            toast.error("Investigation could not be created", { id: toastId, description: detail });
          }
        }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

function Restored({ canonical }: { canonical: CanonicalInvestigation }) {
  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-[calc(100vh-64px)] items-center justify-center bg-slate-950 p-6 text-white">
      <section className="max-w-lg rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-7 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">{canonical.rootContext.label}</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
          The investigation metadata was restored. Reopen from a contextual
          source or start a new investigation to refresh source structures.
        </p>
        <Link
          href="/lattice"
          className="mt-5 inline-flex rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold"
        >
          New investigation
        </Link>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

function Investigation({
  handoffId,
  handoff,
  onStartNew,
  canonical,
}: {
  handoffId: string;
  onStartNew: () => void;
  canonical?: EngineeringIntelligenceInvestigationV1 | null;
  handoff: Extract<HandoffReadResult, { ok: true }>["value"] | LatticeHandoff;
}) {
  const reducedMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const [workspaceFullscreen, setWorkspaceFullscreen] = useState(false);
  const [arranging, setArranging] = useState(false);
  const domain = useMemo(
    () =>
      canonical
        ? buildIntelligenceInvestigation(canonical)
        : buildInvestigation(handoff),
    [canonical, handoff],
  );
  const core = useMemo(() => createInvestigationGraphCore(domain), [domain]);
  const sources = useMemo(
    () => [...new Set(domain.entities.map((entity) => entity.source))],
    [domain.entities],
  );

  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(
    investigationReducer,
    undefined,
    () =>
      loadInvestigation(handoffId, domain, sources) ??
      initialInvestigation(domain.roots, sources),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    saveInvestigation(handoffId, state);
  }, [handoffId, state]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const toggle = () => setWorkspaceFullscreen((value) => !value);
    window.addEventListener("lattice:toggle-workspace-fullscreen", toggle);
    return () =>
      window.removeEventListener("lattice:toggle-workspace-fullscreen", toggle);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const previousBodyOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    const previousHtmlOverflow = document.documentElement.style.overflow;

    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    document.documentElement.style.overflow = "hidden";

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = previousBodyOverflow;
      document.documentElement.style.overflow = previousHtmlOverflow;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!workspaceFullscreen) return;
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") setWorkspaceFullscreen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [workspaceFullscreen]);

  const startNewInvestigation = useCallback(() => {
    dispatch({ type: "start-new-investigation" });
    clearInvestigation(handoffId);
    onStartNew();
  }, [handoffId, onStartNew]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const existing = loadCanonicalInvestigation(`lattice-${handoff.handoffId}`);
    if (!existing) {
      saveCanonicalInvestigation(handoffInvestigationShell(handoff, domain));
    }
  }, [domain, handoff]);

  const projection = useMemo(
    () =>
      projectVisibleGraph(domain, core, {
        expanded: state.interaction.expansion.expanded,
        selection: state.interaction.selection,
        query: state.query,
        focusRoot: state.interaction.expansion.focusRoot,
        sources: state.activeSources,
        relationships: state.activeRelationships,
      }),
    [
      domain,
      core,
      state.interaction.expansion.expanded,
      state.interaction.selection,
      state.interaction.expansion.focusRoot,
      state.query,
      state.activeSources,
      state.activeRelationships,
    ],
  );

  const entity =
    state.interaction.selection.type === "entity"
      ? (domain.byId[state.interaction.selection.id] ?? null)
      : null;
  const relationship =
    state.interaction.selection.type === "relationship"
      ? (domain.relationshipById[state.interaction.selection.id] ?? null)
      : null;
  const related = entity
    ? domain.relationships.filter(
        (item) => item.from === entity.id || item.to === entity.id,
      )
    : [];

  return (
    <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
      <main
        className={
          workspaceFullscreen
            ? "fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#050914] p-3 text-white sm:p-4"
            : "flex h-[calc(100dvh-64px)] min-h-0 flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#050914] p-3 text-white sm:p-4"
        }
      >
        {/* <header className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/75 px-4 py-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={startNewInvestigation}
            aria-label="New investigation"
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 p-2 text-slate-400"
          >
            <IconArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
          <div>
            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-cyan-400">
              Lattice investigation
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
              {handoff.subjectLabel}
            </h1>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
          <span>{domain.entities.length} entities</span>

        <div className="shrink-0">

            sources={sources}

            activeKinds={state.activeRelationships}
            onKind={(value) => dispatch({ type: "relationship", value })}
            focused={Boolean(state.interaction.expansion.focusRoot)}
            onClearFocus={() => dispatch({ type: "focus", id: null })}

        <motion.section
          layout
          className="relative grid min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950"
          style={{
            gridTemplateColumns: state.inspectorOpen
              ? "minmax(0,1fr) 340px"
              : "minmax(0,1fr)",
          }}
          transition={{

                onBilateralReflowStart={() => setArranging(true)}
                onCollapseAll={() => dispatch({ type: "collapse-all" })}
                onSelectEntity={(id) => dispatch({ type: "select-entity", id })}
                onSelectRelationship={(id) =>
                  dispatch({ type: "select-relationship", id })
                }
                onToggle={(id) => dispatch({ type: "toggle", id })}
                onViewport={(value) => dispatch({ type: "viewport", value })}
                onPinPosition={(id, position) =>
                  dispatch({ type: "pin-position", id, position })
                }
                onClearTransient={() => dispatch({ type: "clear-transient" })}
                onEscape={() =>
                  state.inspectorOpen
                    ? dispatch({ type: "close-inspector" })
                    : dispatch({ type: "clear-transient" })
                }
                onNodeAction={({ nodeId, action }) => {
                  if (action === "focus")
                    dispatch({ type: "focus", id: nodeId });
                  else if (
                    action === "expand-one" &&
                    !state.interaction.expansion.expanded.has(nodeId)
                  )
                    dispatch({ type: "toggle", id: nodeId });
                  else if (action === "expand-branch")
                    dispatch({
                      type: "expand-many",
                      ids: [nodeId, ...core.descendants(nodeId)],
                    });
                  else if (action === "collapse-descendants")
                    dispatch({
                      type: "collapse-many",
                      ids: [nodeId, ...core.descendants(nodeId)],
                    });
                  else if (action === "reset-position")
                    dispatch({ type: "unpin-position", id: nodeId });
                  else if (
                    action === "toggle-pin" &&
                    state.interaction.pinnedPositions[nodeId]
                  )
                    dispatch({ type: "unpin-position", id: nodeId });
                  else if (action === "open-details")
                    dispatch({ type: "select-entity", id: nodeId });
                  else if (action === "trace-upstream")
                    dispatch({
                      type: "expand-many",
                      ids: core.ancestors(nodeId),
                    });
                  else if (action === "trace-downstream")
                    dispatch({
                      type: "expand-many",
                      ids: core.descendants(nodeId),
                    });
                  else if (action === "compare-representations")
                    dispatch({ type: "focus", id: domain.roots[0] ?? null });
                }}
                onEdgeAction={({ edgeId, action }) => {
                  const relationship = domain.relationships.find(
                    (item) => item.id === edgeId,
                  );
                  if (!relationship) return;
                  if (action === "hide-family")
                    dispatch({
                      type: "relationship",
                      value: relationship.kind,
                    });
                  else if (action === "trace") {
                    dispatch({
                      type: "expand-many",
                      ids: [
                        relationship.from,
                        relationship.to,
                        ...core.ancestors(relationship.from),
                        ...core.descendants(relationship.to),
                      ],
                    });
                    dispatch({ type: "select-relationship", id: edgeId });
                  } else if (action === "compare-endpoints")
                    dispatch({
                      type: "expand-many",
                      ids: [relationship.from, relationship.to],
                    });
                  else dispatch({ type: "select-relationship", id: edgeId });
                }}
              />
            </LatticeFlowProvider>
          </div>

          <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
            {state.inspectorOpen ? (
              <motion.aside
                key="lattice-inspector-panel"
                id="lattice-inspector-panel"
                initial={
                  reducedMotion ? { opacity: 1 } : { x: 340, opacity: 0 }
                }
                animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }}
                exit={reducedMotion ? { opacity: 0 } : { x: 340, opacity: 0 }}
                transition={{
                  type: "spring",
                  stiffness: 300,
                  damping: 32,
                  mass: 0.85,
                }}
                className="relative min-w-0 overflow-hidden border-l border-slate-800"
              >
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => dispatch({ type: "close-inspector" })}
                  aria-label="Close details"
                  className="absolute right-3 top-3 z-20 rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950/90 p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
                >
                  <IconX className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
                {relationship ? (
                  <RelationshipInspector
                    relationship={relationship}
                    source={domain.byId[relationship.from]}
                    target={domain.byId[relationship.to]}
                  />
                ) : domain.metadata?.contractVersion ? (
                  <IntelligenceInspector graph={domain} entity={entity} />
                ) : (
                  <EntityInspector entity={entity} relationships={related} />
                )}
              </motion.aside>
            ) : null}
          </AnimatePresence>

          {!state.inspectorOpen ? (
            <motion.button
              initial={reducedMotion ? undefined : { x: 16, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ x: 0, opacity: 1 }}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => dispatch({ type: "open-inspector" })}
              aria-expanded="false"
              aria-controls="lattice-inspector-panel"
              className="absolute right-3 top-3 z-20 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950/90 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 shadow-xl"
            >
              <IconChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" /> Details
            </motion.button>
          ) : null}
        </motion.section>
      </main>
    </MotionConfig>
  );
}

function Recovery({ message }: { message: string }) {
  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-[calc(100vh-64px)] items-center justify-center bg-slate-950 p-6 text-white">
      <section className="max-w-md rounded-xl border border-amber-500/25 bg-slate-900 p-7 text-center">
        <IconAlertTriangle className="mx-auto h-7 w-7 text-amber-400" />
        <h1 className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold">
          Contextual launch unavailable
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">{message}</p>
        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-center gap-2">
          <Link
            href="/lattice"
            className="rounded-lg bg-cyan-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold"
          >
            Open Discovery Mode
          </Link>
          <Link
            href="/bom-comparison"
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold"
          >
            BOM Comparison
          </Link>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
